    A non-zero between-group mean indicates either eval
    nondeterminism or a contamination bug in intervention
    state loading at evaluation time.
    within-group BWT measures overwrite-induced interference,
    i.e. forgetting introduced when a later in-group task
    overwrites the shared cluster state that earlier
    in-group tasks contributed to.  This is the controlled
    drift bounded by the KL-anchored fine-tuning step.
